+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+    localResult[li] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) do {                                                   \

+    if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Vector and Triangle Types
 // ============================================================================

 };
 
 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+    localResult[li] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);

+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,      \
+                cudaGetErrorString(cuda_status_));                              \
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);                                           \
+    }                                                                           \
+} while (0)
+
+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+    localResult[li] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};

+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

 };
 
 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+    localResult[li] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

 };
 
 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+    localResult[li] = rho[i] * sum + emission;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

codex
The one- and two-rank executions produce the same result hash and both pass validation, so the distributed ownership and collectives are behaving correctly. Profiling the kernel shape exposed one important performance issue: radiosity currently assigns one GPU thread to an entire matrix row. I’m changing that to one cooperative CUDA block per row so reads are coalesced and the inner sum uses a block reduction.
apply patch

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;

+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+// CUDA dense kernels

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
+
+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) do {                                                   \

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+// CUDA dense kernels

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+// CUDA dense kernels

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+// CUDA dense kernels

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

     state.numTimesteps = timesteps;

/**
 * Room Response Simulation Benchmark
 * 
 * This is a simplified, purely sequential implementation of room impulse response
 * simulation using radiosity-based wave propagation. It models how sound/light
 * waves propagate between surfaces in a room by computing:
 * 
 * 1. Form factors (Kij) between all pairs of triangles based on visibility and geometry
 * 2. Wave propagation using radiosity equations with time delays (Tau)
 * 3. Distance estimation via cross-correlation of radiosity values
 * 
 * The implementation generates a procedural icosphere mesh to simulate a room.
 */

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <random>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// ============================================================================
// Types and Constants
// ============================================================================

using idx_t = uint32_t;
using val_t = float;

constexpr val_t PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
constexpr val_t ZERO = 0.0f;
constexpr val_t ONE = 1.0f;
constexpr val_t WAVE_SPEED = 0.5f;        // Wave propagation speed (units per timestep)
constexpr val_t INV_WAVE_SPEED = 1.0f / WAVE_SPEED;
constexpr int NUM_RAYS = 16;              // Number of rays for visibility sampling
constexpr val_t INV_NUM_RAYS = 1.0f / NUM_RAYS;
constexpr val_t EPSILON = 1e-6f;

// ============================================================================
// Vector and Triangle Types
// ============================================================================

struct Vec3 {
    val_t x, y, z;

    constexpr Vec3() : x(0), y(0), z(0) {}
    constexpr Vec3(val_t x, val_t y, val_t z) : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    explicit constexpr Vec3(val_t v) : x(v), y(v), z(v) {}

    Vec3 operator+(const Vec3& o) const { return {x + o.x, y + o.y, z + o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator-(const Vec3& o) const { return {x - o.x, y - o.y, z - o.z}; }
    Vec3 operator*(val_t s) const { return {x * s, y * s, z * s}; }
    Vec3 operator/(val_t s) const { return {x / s, y / s, z / s}; }
    Vec3 operator-() const { return {-x, -y, -z}; }

    val_t dot(const Vec3& o) const { return x * o.x + y * o.y + z * o.z; }
    Vec3 cross(const Vec3& o) const {
        return {y * o.z - z * o.y, z * o.x - x * o.z, x * o.y - y * o.x};
    }

    val_t squaredNorm() const { return x * x + y * y + z * z; }
    val_t norm() const { return std::sqrt(squaredNorm()); }
    Vec3 normalized() const {
        val_t n = norm();
        return n > EPSILON ? *this / n : Vec3();
    }

    bool operator==(const Vec3& o) const {
        return std::abs(x - o.x) < EPSILON && std::abs(y - o.y) < EPSILON && std::abs(z - o.z) < EPSILON;
    }
};

struct Triangle {
    Vec3 a, b, c;
    Vec3 _normal;

    Triangle() = default;
    Triangle(const Vec3& a, const Vec3& b, const Vec3& c)
        : a(a), b(b), c(c), _normal((b - a).cross(c - a).normalized()) {}

    Vec3 center() const { return (a + b + c) / 3.0f; }
    Vec3 normal() const { return _normal; }

    val_t area() const {
        Vec3 ab = b - a;
        Vec3 ac = c - a;
        return 0.5f * ab.cross(ac).norm();
    }

    bool operator==(const Triangle& o) const { return a == o.a && b == o.b && c == o.c; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Triangle-Box Overlap Test (for octree construction)
// ============================================================================

// Separating axis test for triangle-box overlap
bool triangleBoxOverlap(const Vec3& boxCenter, const Vec3& boxHalfSize, const Triangle& tri) {
    // Translate triangle to box center
    Vec3 v0 = tri.a - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v1 = tri.b - boxCenter;
    Vec3 v2 = tri.c - boxCenter;

    // Triangle edges
    Vec3 e0 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e1 = v2 - v1;
    Vec3 e2 = v0 - v2;

    // Test box normals (AABB axes)
    auto minMax3 = [](val_t a, val_t b, val_t c) {
        return std::make_pair(std::min({a, b, c}), std::max({a, b, c}));
    };

    auto [minX, maxX] = minMax3(v0.x, v1.x, v2.x);
    if (minX > boxHalfSize.x || maxX < -boxHalfSize.x) return false;

    auto [minY, maxY] = minMax3(v0.y, v1.y, v2.y);
    if (minY > boxHalfSize.y || maxY < -boxHalfSize.y) return false;

    auto [minZ, maxZ] = minMax3(v0.z, v1.z, v2.z);
    if (minZ > boxHalfSize.z || maxZ < -boxHalfSize.z) return false;

    // Test triangle normal
    Vec3 triNormal = e0.cross(e1);
    val_t d = triNormal.dot(v0);
    val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(triNormal.x) +
              boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(triNormal.y) +
              boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(triNormal.z);
    if (std::abs(d) > r) return false;

    // Test 9 edge cross products
    auto testAxis = [&](const Vec3& axis) {
        val_t p0 = axis.dot(v0);
        val_t p1 = axis.dot(v1);
        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);
        val_t r = boxHalfSize.x * std::abs(axis.x) +
                  boxHalfSize.y * std::abs(axis.y) +
                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
    };

    Vec3 axes[3] = {{1,0,0}, {0,1,0}, {0,0,1}};
    Vec3 edges[3] = {e0, e1, e2};
    for (const auto& axis : axes) {
        for (const auto& edge : edges) {
            Vec3 crossAxis = axis.cross(edge);
            if (crossAxis.squaredNorm() > EPSILON) {
                if (!testAxis(crossAxis)) return false;
            }
        }
    }

    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Octree for Spatial Acceleration
// ============================================================================

constexpr size_t MAX_OCTREE_TRIS = 8;
constexpr val_t MAX_OCTREE_LEAF_SIZE = 0.5f;

class Octree {
public:
    Vec3 minBound, maxBound;
    Vec3 halfExtent, center;
    std::unique_ptr<Octree> children[8];
    std::vector<size_t> triangleIndices;  // Indices into the global triangle list
    const std::vector<Triangle>* allTriangles;  // Pointer to all triangles

    Octree() : allTriangles(nullptr) {}

    void build(const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles) {
        allTriangles = &triangles;
        if (triangles.empty()) return;

        // Compute bounding box
        minBound = triangles[0].a;
        maxBound = triangles[0].a;
        for (const auto& tri : triangles) {
            for (const auto* v : {&tri.a, &tri.b, &tri.c}) {
                minBound.x = std::min(minBound.x, v->x);
                minBound.y = std::min(minBound.y, v->y);
                minBound.z = std::min(minBound.z, v->z);
                maxBound.x = std::max(maxBound.x, v->x);
                maxBound.y = std::max(maxBound.y, v->y);
                maxBound.z = std::max(maxBound.z, v->z);
            }
        }

        // Collect all indices
        std::vector<size_t> allIndices(triangles.size());
        for (size_t i = 0; i < triangles.size(); ++i) allIndices[i] = i;

        buildNode(allIndices, minBound, maxBound);
    }

private:
    void buildNode(const std::vector<size_t>& indices, const Vec3& nodeMin, const Vec3& nodeMax) {
        minBound = nodeMin;
        maxBound = nodeMax;
        halfExtent = (maxBound - minBound) * 0.5f;

+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);

+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+// ============================================================================
 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) do {                                                   \
+    cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (call);                                          \
+    if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,      \
+                cudaGetErrorString(cuda_status_));                              \
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);                                           \

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}
+

-    std::mt19937 rng;

+    val_t rand() {

+    }

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

+struct ParallelContext {

+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;

+static ParallelContext parallelContext;

+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================
