+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

 int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    int provided = 0;
+    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);
+    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &parallelContext.rank);
+    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &parallelContext.size);
+    if (provided < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
+        if (parallelContext.rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "MPI lacks required thread support\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3);
+    }
+    MPI_Comm localComm;
+    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, parallelContext.rank,
+                        MPI_INFO_NULL, &localComm);
+    MPI_Comm_rank(localComm, &parallelContext.localRank);

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+// CUDA dense kernels

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};

+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // Initialization
 // ============================================================================

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call) do {                                                   \
+    cudaError_t cuda_status_ = (call);                                          \
+    if (cuda_status_ != cudaSuccess) {                                          \
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\n", __FILE__, __LINE__,      \

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+        return static_cast<val_t>(z >> 40) * (1.0f / 16777216.0f);
+    }

 // ============================================================================

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;

+static ParallelContext parallelContext;

+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA parallelization while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI distributes triangle-row workloads and synchronizes matrices/radiosity.
- OpenMP parallelizes form-factor visibility calculations and initialization.
- CUDA accelerates time delays, wave propagation, and cross-correlation.
- MPI ranks are automatically mapped to node-local GPUs.
- Deterministic sampling produces identical results across rank/thread configurations.
- CMake now requires and unconditionally enables CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP.
- No new source files or dependencies were added.

Validation completed successfully for:

- 1 MPI rank / 4 OpenMP threads
- 2 MPI ranks / 2 OpenMP threads
- Default 320-triangle workload with 2 ranks / 4 threads
- Identical hashes across one- and two-rank runs

Modified files:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)

+struct ParallelContext {
+    int rank = 0;
+    int size = 1;
+    int localRank = 0;
+    size_t rowBegin = 0;
+    size_t rowEnd = 0;
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+};
+
+static ParallelContext parallelContext;
+
+static void setRowPartition(size_t n) {
+    const size_t q = n / static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    const size_t r = n % static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.rowBegin = static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) * q +
+        std::min(static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank), r);
+    parallelContext.rowEnd = parallelContext.rowBegin + q +
+        (static_cast<size_t>(parallelContext.rank) < r ? 1 : 0);
+    parallelContext.counts.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    parallelContext.displacements.resize(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p) {
+        size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(p) * q +
+            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(p), r);
+        size_t rows = q + (static_cast<size_t>(p) < r ? 1 : 0);
+        parallelContext.counts[p] = static_cast<int>(rows);
+        parallelContext.displacements[p] = static_cast<int>(begin);
+    }
+}

 // ============================================================================
+// CUDA dense kernels
+// ============================================================================

+__global__ void timeDelayKernel(const Triangle* triangles, int* localTau,
+                                size_t n, size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t k = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total = localRows * n;
+    if (k >= total) return;
+    size_t li = k / n;
+    size_t j = k - li * n;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    if (i == j) {
+        localTau[k] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    const Triangle& a = triangles[i];
+    const Triangle& b = triangles[j];
+    val_t ax = (a.a.x + a.b.x + a.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t ay = (a.a.y + a.b.y + a.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t az = (a.a.z + a.b.z + a.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bx = (b.a.x + b.b.x + b.c.x) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t by = (b.a.y + b.b.y + b.c.y) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t bz = (b.a.z + b.b.z + b.c.z) * (1.0f / 3.0f);
+    val_t dx = ax - bx, dy = ay - by, dz = az - bz;
+    localTau[k] = __float2int_ru(sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz) * INV_WAVE_SPEED);
+}
+
+__global__ void propagationKernel(const val_t* kij, const int* tau,
+                                  const val_t* areas, const val_t* rho,
+                                  val_t* radB, val_t* localResult,
+                                  size_t n, size_t t, size_t rowBegin,
+                                  size_t localRows, size_t sourceIndex,
+                                  size_t timeOff) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t sum = ZERO;
+    size_t row = i * n;
+    for (size_t j = threadIdx.x; j < n; j += blockDim.x) {
+        int delay = tau[row + j];
+        if (i == j || static_cast<int>(t) < delay) continue;
+        val_t factor = kij[row + j];
+        if (factor <= ZERO) continue;
+        val_t r = radB[(t - static_cast<size_t>(delay)) * n + j];
+        if (r > ZERO) sum += fminf(factor * areas[j], ONE) * r;
+    }
+    __shared__ val_t partial[256];
+    partial[threadIdx.x] = sum;
+    __syncthreads();
+    for (unsigned stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+        if (threadIdx.x < stride) partial[threadIdx.x] += partial[threadIdx.x + stride];
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        val_t emission = (i == sourceIndex && t < timeOff) ? ONE : ZERO;
+        localResult[li] = rho[i] * partial[0] + emission;
+    }
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const val_t* radB, val_t* localDistances,
+                               size_t n, size_t timesteps, size_t sourceIndex,
+                               size_t rowBegin, size_t localRows) {
+    size_t li = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (li >= localRows) return;
+    size_t i = rowBegin + li;
+    val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
+    size_t bestT = 0;
+    for (size_t lag = 0; lag < timesteps; ++lag) {
+        val_t sum = ZERO;
+        for (size_t tt = lag; tt < timesteps; ++tt)
+            sum += radB[tt * n + i] * radB[(tt - lag) * n + sourceIndex];
+        if (sum > maxCorr) {
+            maxCorr = sum;
+            bestT = lag;
+        }
+    }
+    localDistances[li] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledCounts(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.counts[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+
+static std::vector<int> scaledDisplacements(size_t scale) {
+    std::vector<int> result(parallelContext.size);
+    for (int p = 0; p < parallelContext.size; ++p)
+        result[p] = parallelContext.displacements[p] * static_cast<int>(scale);
+    return result;
+}
+

 // ============================================================================

tokens used
65,589
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA parallelization while preserving the `roomsim` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI distributes triangle-row workloads and synchronizes matrices/radiosity.
- OpenMP parallelizes form-factor visibility calculations and initialization.
- CUDA accelerates time delays, wave propagation, and cross-correlation.
- MPI ranks are automatically mapped to node-local GPUs.
- Deterministic sampling produces identical results across rank/thread configurations.
- CMake now requires and unconditionally enables CUDA, MPI, and OpenMP.
- No new source files or dependencies were added.

Validation completed successfully for:

- 1 MPI rank / 4 OpenMP threads
- 2 MPI ranks / 2 OpenMP threads
- Default 320-triangle workload with 2 ranks / 4 threads
- Identical hashes across one- and two-rank runs

Modified files:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r5/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)
